.ai/DECISIONS.md:3992:Decision: `D-2026-10-09-s2-rotation-reconciliation-followup`. Codex Architect, resolving S2-02 Q37–Q40 and fix re-review R3 in `.agent/architect-pre-S3-answers.md` after S2 closeout; this is a follow-up contract, not a reopened S2 verdict. For the same-account pilot, `ISSUE_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN` may be a supervised operator halt without a local marker; a later successful issue on a readable DB creates a new protected file and revokes earlier selectors atomically. A marker alone is not a trusted second authority. The current fresh *unlocked* no-selector read is **not conclusive proof of rollback**: the original transaction may still finish its COMMIT. Before any non-demo/unattended rotation or real pilot, a bounded S2-02r correction must reconcile under the same gateway-row `FOR UPDATE` lock used by issuance, wait for any prior transaction to finish, then check selector/verifier, credential reference, expected source enablement and survival of the previously active credential in that locked transaction. Only proven non-commit with the old credential active permits deleting the new file; timeout or inconsistent state keeps the protected file and typed unknown outcome. `pg_stat_activity` alone does not establish this ordering. Distinguish a provably pre-transaction failure from an ambiguous COMMIT in that follow-up; the present conservative unknown result is a carry, not secret loss. The v3 store-root-bound comparison correctly closes on older servers until restart; its hash of canonical resolved Evidence/commit roots is accepted, and a moved store needs a fresh report. S2-02r is not an S3 analytic prerequisite.

.ai/DECISIONS.md:4144:Decision: `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort`. Accept LR-01 Q1-Q4 as corrected by Follow-up M1 in `.agent/architect-LR-01-answers.md`. Source-time order requires a versioned, scoped clock attestation from an identified authority covering each reading with maximum UTC error no greater than 60 seconds; simulated proof stays separate from real-source proof. Order an attested channel by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) +/- the attested error, not by the full measurement interval widened by error. Measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap; genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remains `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous. Where placement-uncertainty intervals are disjoint, gateway admission sequence disagreement is a late arrival, not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`; sequence still resolves identity and duplicates. Retain epoch ambiguity unless the existing explicit cohort selection is used; automatic cross-epoch continuity needs separate review. Attestation changes enqueue all affected UTC-day windows through the matching six-hour query bracket and neighboring continuity windows, preserving immutable runs and selections. 4116-Addendum to `D-2026-10-13-s4-bounded-financial-basis` (S4-02b `fuel-cost-1`): The first reviewed fuel model may use immutable, dimensionless `sfc_upper_multiplier = 1.10` as an expressly conditional assumption that actual L/kWh does not exceed 1.10 times the selected Foundation SFC. Its version records the value, inequality, scope, source, reviewer/date, formula and immutable lineage; it is not a measured or calibrated physical bound. For each exact FindingRevision, quantify only observed Candidate spans fully covered by accepted, good, nonoverlapping generator-power mean intervals, each wholly contained within contiguous Candidate time and carrying one unambiguous positive SFC throughout. Count each distinct interval once: `quantity_low = 0 L`, `quantity_high = 1.10 × Σ(mean kW × full interval hours × SFC L/kWh)`. Never use dispatch interval `upper_minutes`, a segment `max mean × duration`, or prorating of an interval that crosses non-Candidate time. Any failed coverage, containment, power or SFC condition makes the whole consequence `QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE` with no amount. The upper is a model-conditioned potential fuel-cost exposure, not measured fuel saved or a physical maximum. Independent model review and the planned owner UI checkpoint still apply. Rulings: `.agent/architect-S4-02-answers.md` (Q1, plan review C4).

4122-`SiteWindowSummary` is a tenant/Site-scoped, selected half-open UTC window read/projection over committed public records. It identifies the exact selected S3 assessment revision and its source/configuration/evidence basis, eligible S4 Finding revisions and any bounded financial consequence revisions, with accepted-source and analytic watermarks, model version, built time, coverage and a typed freshness reason. A user date change selects a new window; reload preserves the requested window and pinned detail IDs. A projection is CURRENT only when its accepted-source basis matches the ledger and relevant analytic/consequence work is complete. Preserve old exact revision links when a new result is pending or stale. `UNASSESSED`/Unknown means no completed assessment or insufficient input, `PENDING` means first computation or queued work with no current result, `STALE_PROJECTION` means an earlier result exists but its watermark/basis lags or work is dirty, and `FAILED` names failed work without a usable current result. None implies Healthy, zero cost or no Findings.

4134-Action state is derived from an append-only, server-ordered `ActionEvent` ledger. Record stable event ID, Action and Site, event type, `occurred_at`, server `recorded_at`, idempotency key, actor kind/ID, source channel, authorization basis and triggering command/job or content revision. Idempotent identical retries return the event; conflicting reuse and cross-Site references fail. Historical reads select a recorded cutoff and then `(recorded_at,id)` order; a backdated `occurred_at` is displayed as such but never rewrites the audit order. `PROPOSED`/`ACCEPTED`, application `PENDING`/`APPLIED`/`FAILED`, and later verification status are separate dimensions. Acceptance does not assert application; an applied change or completed work does not assert benefit. A failed application preserves its attempt, error and retry relation without an effective-policy claim. No human author, approval or attestation is inferred from a gateway credential, client field, local OS user or SYSTEM fixture. Under the existing `A-actor-identity` rule, human writes need a verified operator principal; otherwise only a clearly labelled, server-authorized SYSTEM demonstration command with stable service/job provenance is allowed, or the write stays unavailable.

19-**Store provenance at ingestion.** A Site/day match to an authored spec is not proof that a particular accepted reading was authored for demonstration. The current label is acceptable for the reviewed, isolated S4-02 demonstration and its fail-closed `Source not confirmed` behavior, but it can falsely label a real KOBO-001 Finding on 2026-10-23 and is not tenant scoped. Add an immutable, tenant/Site-scoped, server-authorized batch/publication source designation with fixture/spec version and publication identity; bind it to accepted evidence IDs and carry the cited evidence's designation into Finding/consequence reads. Gateway credentials or client-declared text alone must not confer authorship. Mixed or unresolvable inputs must show a cautious source state, not claim that the whole Finding is demonstration data or real measurements. Retire the date heuristic once this path is live.
20-
21-**Code:** yes, a source-provenance follow-on spanning S2 ingestion and S4 reads/UI. It must land **before any real-Site pilot and before W1 exit**; until then the current demonstration label is evidence only for the bounded S4-02 fixture, not a general source-authorship capability. **Owner input:** ask the owner to approve the visible wording for mixed/unknown provenance and the pilot data designation at the W1 checkpoint. The need for stored provenance is an architecture requirement, not conditional on that copy choice.
22-
23:## Q4 — `assumptions:3#a` and consequence history
24-
25-**Per-Finding versions do not satisfy the registered claim.** `assumptions:3#a` says *prior assumption and consequence revisions at selected cutoff* on the Assumptions interpretation-history surface. The built Assumptions view selects price history at a recorded cutoff; Overview lists consequence versions only for one selected FindingRevision, with exact price links and no consequence cutoff selection. The latter is valid `I-consequence-history-read` evidence for a per-Finding read, but it does not answer which Site consequences used a price or what the Site knew at the chosen cutoff. Exact-ID links alone do not provide that query.
26-
27:**Code:** yes, an S4-02 Assumptions-history follow-on before `assumptions:3#a` promotion and W1 exit. Add a tenant/Site-scoped, paged consequence-history read at the selected recorded cutoff, with exact Finding/FindingRevision/consequence and price revision IDs, recorded/valid times, historical/current-at-cutoff distinction, and typed missing/closed-source states. The Assumptions view must expose those versions beside or from the selected price history, with an exact link back to the Finding; no fabricated estate total is implied. Review cutoff boundaries, pagination, foreign-Site refusal and app/mock states.
28-
29-**Registry closeout now:** after recording the accepted S4-02b reviews and owner checkpoint, the coordinator may resolve the supported S4-02 storage, financial-basis, per-Finding read/history and UI nodes and `I-S4-02b`; `I-S4-02` may resolve as the reviewed a/b task only if its acceptance text is corrected so it no longer promises promotion of four supported W1 portions. G-05 may still track the separate non-core Assumptions and Financials mock omissions. No owner decision is needed to preserve the registered claim; the owner should see the partial W1 status.

USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: false (non-UI; no owner-facing copy or visual change)

## Outcome and authority

A server-authorized SYSTEM publication may replace the sole open-ended future Site price exactly at that assignment's `valid_from`. The previous scheduled assignment remains visible at earlier recorded cutoffs; the current validity timeline contains only the replacement from that instant. This implements `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md` Q1 and the S4-02a assumption contract. Independent of unmerged LR-01, S5-01, S6-01 and S4-05 branches.

## Code reality and boundary

- `backend/assetops_backend/financials/assumptions.py:193-217`: `plan_assignments` currently requires `only.valid_from < valid_from` and always inserts a left remainder; equality fails. Keep `valid_from >= recorded_at`, exactly one overlap, and open-ended prior requirements.
- `backend/assetops_backend/financials/adapters/postgres_assumption_store.py:99-176`: the sole assumption writer validates SYSTEM, serializes a Site/key with an advisory transaction lock, checks idempotency before expected prior, takes DB time after the lock, closes recorded tenure, then inserts planned assignments in one transaction. Its `snapshot` at `:195-222` selects prior recorded cutoffs.
- `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1:641-645,732-756`: rule 8 confines assumption inserts and the set-once `recorded_to` update to that writer. Preserve this ownership. No migration is expected; if one is needed, re-chain from main head 0013 against then-merged migrations (LR-01 adds 0014/0015 on its branch; S6-01a/S4-05a may add more).

## Build and acceptance

1. Change the planner only for equality with the sole open-ended overlapping assignment: close its recorded tenure and insert the new revision's `[valid_from, infinity)` assignment, with no zero-length left row. Later-start SUCCESSION, INITIAL and CORRECTION retain their reviewed behavior. Keep bounded or multiple overlap and `valid_from < recorded_at` as 409; authorization remains server-side SYSTEM.
2. Real-Postgres proof publishes an INITIAL future price, then a SUCCESSION at exactly its start with the current expected prior. Assert one current assignment, exact boundary continuity, no empty interval, the original scheduled price and assignment at a cutoff before publication, and the replacement at a cutoff after it. Assert revision/assignment IDs and `recorded_to`/`recorded_from` agree with those cutoffs.
3. Retry the identical request/key: return the same publication without another revision/assignment. Reuse its key with changed content, use a stale expected prior, and race two publishers using the same prior: one commits; the other gets 409 and leaves no extra row. Include >1 overlapping and bounded-prior refusal. Verify unchanged earlier-price consequence selection when its window does not cross the new start.

Focused checks: `backend/tests/test_s4_assumptions_postgres.py` and relevant consequence price-selection tests; `tools/check-all.ps1`. On the final revision run one real-Postgres full backend suite using `backend/TESTING.md` (xdist `-n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder`, JUnit zero skipped/failed, keep-awake, 20-minute cap), then give the reviewer the same-revision evidence. No UI walkthrough or owner checkpoint.

## Architect questions

None required to build. Recommendation: retain the existing SUCCESSION conflict codes and expose the earlier recorded-cutoff assignment through the existing history read; Q1 already decides equality semantics.

## Outcome and authority

Resolve `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1 and `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` late-reading carries L-aâ€“L-f, L1 and the S4-02b panel residual under decision `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`. Run 1 currently yields S3 `INDETERMINATE` / `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` / `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS`, a revised `SUPPORTED` Finding ending at 07:00 with only `OPEN_BOUND`, then `CURRENT PRICED` 7.5625 USD. The on-time-sequenced late upload yields 15.8125 USD. Neither figure is an automatic current-money claim when its generator series is unresolved. Keep the existing authored fixture `backend/conversions/s4-power-late-demo-cases-v1.json:2-13` and accepted consequence history.

## Architect-ruled contract â€” S3 cohort v4

The reviewed contract is deterministic and fail closed:

1. For each selected `(gateway, source, sequence_epoch, role, asset)` channel, use **source-time order** only when a durable, versioned, tenant/Site/source/epoch-scoped clock attestation (new) proves the source clock's maximum UTC error at the reading times is â‰¤60 seconds, identifies its authority and validity interval, and covers every reading in the proposed ordering. A labelled simulated fixture can carry an explicit, separately identified simulated attestation; simulated proof cannot validate a real source and `source_mode=SIMULATED` alone proves no clock. Persist the attestation ID/version in each analytic input basis. A real gateway lacking this proof retains `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED` and an unresolved series limit.
2. For an attested channel, order by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) Â± the attested UTC error, **not** by the whole measurement interval widened by error. Back-to-back measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap. Genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remain `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous, even if gateway sequence agrees; do not choose a winner or interpolate. With disjoint placement-uncertainty intervals, source-time order wins even if admission sequence disagrees: a late arrival is not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`. Dense in-order five-minute means must remain usable. A reading outside attestation validity, across an unproved clock step, or spanning conflicting attestations remains limited. The 60-second cap is an admission ceiling, not a claim about this gateway.
3. Never merge sequence epochs by source time alone. The existing multi-epoch default ambiguity and explicit cohort-selection path remain; automatic continuity across epochs is outside LR-01 and needs a separately reviewed public proof. Sequence still authenticates identity/duplication, not source-time order. Keep every refused span and the involved evidence/attestation IDs in the input digest and assessment limitations. Use a new S3 `MODEL_VERSION` and a new S4 `MATCHER_VERSION`; preserve old runs, selections, assessment and Finding revisions.

import { Disclosure } from "../ui";

/**
 * S4-05b phase 1: the stored exact-revision source provenance every Finding and money read carries
 * (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py`, S4-05 shared contract item 3).
 *
 * The source label comes only from the server's stored binding of the exact accepted evidence IDs, never from a
 * date, a Site or a spec file. Labels and statements follow D-2026-10-16-s405b-label (interim; the owner's W1
 * copy checkpoint sets final wording). Label and statement are always visible; identifiers, publications and
 * reasons sit in the closed source disclosure in plain words; reason codes stay in `data-source-reason`.
 */

export type SourceState = "AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION" | "REGISTERED_SIMULATION" | "MIXED" | "UNKNOWN";

export interface SourceReason {
  code: string;
  accepted_evidence_ids: string[];
  [detail: string]: unknown;
}

export interface SourceEvidenceLink {
  accepted_evidence_id: string;
  status: string;
  batch_id: string | null;
  publication_id: string | null;
  source_class: string | null;
  fixture_id: string | null;
  fixture_version: string | null;
  run_id: string | null;
  run_identity_sha256: string | null;
  broken_detail: string | null;
}

export interface SourceProvenance {
  tenant_id: string;
  site_id: string;
  finding_revision_id: string | null;
  consequence_revision_id: string | null;
  cited_accepted_evidence_ids: string[];
  power_accepted_evidence_ids: string[] | null;
  finding_revision_state?: SourceState;
  state: SourceState;
  reasons: SourceReason[];
  accepted_evidence_ids: string[];
  problematic_accepted_evidence_ids: string[];
  publication_ids: string[];
  source_classes: string[];
  fixtures: { fixture_id: string; fixture_version: string }[];
  runs: { run_id: string; run_identity_sha256: string }[];
  evidence: SourceEvidenceLink[];
}

/** The pair a money read names: the selected Finding version's set and the consequence version's union. */
export interface SourceProvenancePair {
  finding_revision: SourceProvenance | null;
  consequence_revision: SourceProvenance | null;
}

export const SOURCE_WORDS: Record<SourceState, { label: string; statement: string }> = {
  AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION: {
    label: "Prepared example data",
    statement: "These records were prepared for an example. They are not readings from physical equipment.",
  },
  REGISTERED_SIMULATION: {
    label: "Generated test data",
    statement: "These records were generated for a test. They are not readings from physical equipment.",
  },
  MIXED: {
    label: "Sources do not agree",
    statement: "The records behind this result have different or unverified sources. No current cost is shown.",
  },
  UNKNOWN: {
    label: "Source unverified",
    statement: "We cannot establish the source of all records behind this result. No current cost is shown.",
  },
};

/** Plain words for each typed reason; the code itself stays in `data-source-reason`. */
const REASON_WORDS: Record<string, string> = {
  EMPTY_CITATION_SET: "This version cites no records.",
  EVIDENCE_NOT_FOUND: "Some cited records could not be found.",
  FOREIGN_SITE_EVIDENCE: "Some cited records belong to another site.",
  BROKEN_PUBLICATION_LINK: "The source record of some readings is damaged or does not match them.",
  NO_POSITIVE_BINDING: "No reading has a confirmed source.",
  BOUND_AND_UNBOUND: "Some readings have a confirmed source and some do not.",
  CROSSED_SOURCE_CLASSES: "Prepared example records and generated test records are combined.",
  AUTHORED_FIXTURE_VERSION_CONFLICT: "Records from different prepared examples are combined.",
  MULTIPLE_RUNS_WITHOUT_COMPATIBLE_BASIS: "Readings from more than one generated data set are combined.",
  RUN_IDENTITY_CONFLICT: "One generated data set is recorded with conflicting identities.",
  CONSEQUENCE_POWER_INPUT_CONFLICT: "The generator readings used for the cost do not share the finding's source.",
  PROVENANCE_NOT_WIRED: "The source record could not be read.",
  PROVENANCE_UNREADABLE: "The source record could not be read.",
  PROVENANCE_REVISION_NOT_FOUND: "The source record could not be read.",
};

export function reasonWords(code: string): string {
  return REASON_WORDS[code] ?? "Another source problem was recorded.";
}

/** The state a reader is shown; a missing block is unknown, never positive. */
export function sourceState(p: SourceProvenance | null | undefined): SourceState {
  // Review L2: a state this client does not know (e.g. a later attested class) reads as unverified, never throws.
  const state = p?.state;
  return state !== undefined && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SOURCE_WORDS, state) ? state : "UNKNOWN";
}

  if (ids.length === 0) return null;

## Architect ruling

Questions answered by `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux` in `.agent/architect-W1-ux-answers.md`. Substantive deeper material belongs in its owning contextual destination, available as the same named tab or view link at 1440 and 390 px. The closed â€œHow this was worked outâ€ disclosure holds only a few labelled exact references. Site, Evidence, Finding, Action, Verification and Analysis are approved domain/destination words with nearby first-use explanations; labels never grant a positive claim. Default prose, graphics and accessibility text exclude raw codes, IDs and implementation vocabulary; Analysis/Evidence prose follows the same rule, while labelled exact reference data remains available. Authored/test data stays explicitly qualified. Honest selected-read state outranks mock presentation.

 notice('Acceptance creates managed work. It does not close the Finding, verify the change or send a real controller command.','blue')+
    def chain(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, finding_id: UUID, finding_revision_id: UUID, *,
              consequence_revision_id: UUID | None, after_revision_no: int, limit: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """One snapshot of a FindingRevision's consequence chain: its head, an optional exact revision, a
        keyset page, the lineage links of every returned revision and the pending work that touches it.

        Pending work touches the read when it names this Finding (a FindingRevision append, or S4-02c accepted
        evidence: matched by Finding, its queued revision is only a locator kept for diagnostics) or when an
        assumption publication's affected range overlaps the FindingRevision's window."""
        scope = {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id, "f": finding_id, "r": finding_revision_id,
                 "k": fc.CONSEQUENCE_TYPE}

        def work(c: Connection):
            base = ("SELECT * FROM financial_consequence_revision WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s "
                    "AND finding_id = :f AND finding_revision_id = :r AND consequence_type = :k ")
            head = c.execute(text(base + "ORDER BY revision_no DESC LIMIT 1"), scope).first()
            exact = None if consequence_revision_id is None else c.execute(
                text(base + "AND id = :c"), {**scope, "c": consequence_revision_id}).first()
            page = c.execute(text(base + "AND revision_no > :after ORDER BY revision_no LIMIT :n"),
                             {**scope, "after": after_revision_no, "n": limit + 1}).all()
            total = c.execute(text(base.replace("SELECT *", "SELECT count(*)")), scope).scalar_one()
            rows = [dict(x._mapping) for x in page]
            ids = list({x["id"] for x in rows} | {x.id for x in (head, exact) if x is not None})
            links: dict[str, dict[Any, list[dict[str, Any]]]] = {}
            for name, table, order in (
                    ("assessments", "financial_consequence_assessment_revision",
                     "is_primary DESC, assessment_revision_id"),
                    ("assumptions", "financial_consequence_assumption_revision", "assumption_revision_id"),
                    ("intervals", "financial_consequence_power_interval", "interval_start, evidence_id")):
                out: dict[Any, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
                for x in c.execute(text(f"SELECT * FROM {table} WHERE tenant_id = :t AND site_id = :s "
                                        f"AND consequence_revision_id = ANY(:ids) ORDER BY {order}"),
                                   {**scope, "ids": ids}):
                    out.setdefault(x.consequence_revision_id, []).append(dict(x._mapping))
                links[name] = out
            window = c.execute(text("SELECT episode_start, episode_end FROM finding_revision WHERE tenant_id = :t "
                                    "AND site_id = :s AND id = :r"), scope).first()
            pending = [] if window is None else [dict(x._mapping) for x in c.execute(text(
                "SELECT w.id, w.cause, w.assumption_revision_id, w.finding_revision_id, w.accepted_evidence_id, "
                "w.affected_from, "
                "w.affected_to, w.enqueued_at, (SELECT count(*) FROM financial_consequence_work_attempt a "
                "WHERE a.work_id = w.id) AS attempts, (SELECT a.error_code FROM financial_consequence_work_attempt a "
                "WHERE a.work_id = w.id ORDER BY a.attempt_no DESC LIMIT 1) AS last_error_code "
                "FROM financial_consequence_work w WHERE w.tenant_id = :t AND w.site_id = :s "
                "AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM financial_consequence_work_attempt a WHERE a.work_id = w.id "
                "AND a.outcome = 'SUCCEEDED') AND ((w.cause IN ('FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED', 'EVIDENCE_ACCEPTED') "
                "AND w.finding_id = :f) "
                "OR (w.cause = 'ASSUMPTION_PUBLISHED' AND (w.affected_to IS NULL OR w.affected_to > :lo) "
                "AND w.affected_from < :hi)) ORDER BY w.enqueued_at, w.id"),
                {**scope, "lo": window[0], "hi": window[1]})]
            return {"head": None if head is None else dict(head._mapping),
                    "exact": None if exact is None else dict(exact._mapping),
                    "page": rows[:limit], "has_more": len(rows) > limit, "total": total, "links": links,
                    "pending": pending}
        return self._run(work, "REPEATABLE READ")

    # ---- raw rows (tests and diagnostics) -------------------------------------------------------------------

    def rows(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:

+# S4-08 — Site assumption and consequence history
+
+Status: implementation-ready. Size S: one read and one UI view, one build phase and fresh independent review. Branch for implementation: `task/S4-08-assumption-history` from reviewed main. Planning branch: `plan/S4-08-assumption-history`. Coordinator commits this packet.
+
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true. Under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`, fresh independent slice review (and fixes) is the merge gate; the owner reviews this screen in the combined W1 walkthrough, not a per-slice session. Registry: `I-assumption-consequence-history-read`, `I-assumption-consequence-history-ui`, `I-S4-08`; core portion `assumptions:3#a` remains open until implementation is reviewed. Authority: `D-2026-10-14-s402-post-review-follow-ons`, `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md` Q4 and `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Outcome and code reality
+
+At one selected recorded cutoff, a Site reader can see its price timeline and every recorded consequence version that used a price, including earlier versions, exact lineage and a link to the owning Finding. No Site or estate sum follows from these rows. The v69 `assumptions` screen (`frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:485-490`) supplies the price/history composition; its editable price, maintenance accrual, preview and illustrative values are not authorized product data.
+
+| Existing seam (unless **new**) | S4-08 work |
+| --- | --- |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/assumption_read.py:3-5,63-96` and `financial_read_api.py:64-85` select a Site price timeline at `recorded_at`, with keyset price paging. S4-06 preserves equal-start succession at prior cutoffs (`financials/assumptions.py:193-217`). | Reuse the exact UTC cutoff and URL selector. Keep price selection independent of consequence paging. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/adapters/postgres_consequence_store.py:381-436` reads a single FindingRevision chain; `financials/consequence_read.py:169-268` exposes exact versions, source provenance and money gates. `finding_api.py:245-268` scopes exact links. | **New** Site-wide, read-only consequence-history query and API; do not stitch the UI from per-Finding requests. Reuse exact row DTO, scope and gate semantics where sound. |
+| `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-123,143-152` stores tenant/Site, Finding/Revision, price link, window and `recorded_at`; main head `0019_verification_core`. | Existing immutable rows and links support this query. No migration unless the implementer demonstrates a missing stored fact or required index with query proof and Architect review. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py:23-36,60-83`, `financials/consequence_read.py:217-254` and `frontend/src/sites/sourceProvenance.tsx:1-94` resolve S4-05b source labels per exact consequence version; LR-01 eligibility and currentness also gate money. | **New** per-row provenance and current-money disposition, using stored accepted-evidence bindings. Never infer source from Site/day, price, or the mock. |
+| `frontend/src/sites/SiteFinancials.tsx:192-231,356-390` preserves Assumptions cutoff/price page in the URL. | **New** consequence-history panel or contextual view reachable from selected price history; maintain the same cutoff through paging, Finding links and reload. |
+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware and cannot be future. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+
+Combined W1 walkthrough map: the existing `assumptions` row in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` now includes the S4-08 guarded case and selected `recorded_at` URL. Show prior/current-at-cut versions, missing/withheld/closed-source states, exact Finding link and source label after reload. The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

+## Outcome and code reality
+
+At one selected recorded cutoff, a Site reader can see its price timeline and every recorded consequence version that used a price, including earlier versions, exact lineage and a link to the owning Finding. No Site or estate sum follows from these rows. The v69 `assumptions` screen (`frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:485-490`) supplies the price/history composition; its editable price, maintenance accrual, preview and illustrative values are not authorized product data.
+
+| Existing seam (unless **new**) | S4-08 work |
+| --- | --- |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/assumption_read.py:3-5,63-96` and `financial_read_api.py:64-85` select a Site price timeline at `recorded_at`, with keyset price paging. S4-06 preserves equal-start succession at prior cutoffs (`financials/assumptions.py:193-217`). | Reuse the exact UTC cutoff and URL selector. Keep price selection independent of consequence paging. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/adapters/postgres_consequence_store.py:381-436` reads a single FindingRevision chain; `financials/consequence_read.py:169-268` exposes exact versions, source provenance and money gates. `finding_api.py:245-268` scopes exact links. | **New** Site-wide, read-only consequence-history query and API; do not stitch the UI from per-Finding requests. Reuse exact row DTO, scope and gate semantics where sound. |
+| `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-123,143-152` stores tenant/Site, Finding/Revision, price link, window and `recorded_at`; main head `0019_verification_core`. | Existing immutable rows and links support this query. No migration unless the implementer demonstrates a missing stored fact or required index with query proof and Architect review. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py:23-36,60-83`, `financials/consequence_read.py:217-254` and `frontend/src/sites/sourceProvenance.tsx:1-94` resolve S4-05b source labels per exact consequence version; LR-01 eligibility and currentness also gate money. | **New** per-row provenance and current-money disposition, using stored accepted-evidence bindings. Never infer source from Site/day, price, or the mock. |
+| `frontend/src/sites/SiteFinancials.tsx:192-231,356-390` preserves Assumptions cutoff/price page in the URL. | **New** consequence-history panel or contextual view reachable from selected price history; maintain the same cutoff through paging, Finding links and reload. |
+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware and cannot be future. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+
+Combined W1 walkthrough map: the existing `assumptions` row in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` now includes the S4-08 guarded case and selected `recorded_at` URL. Show prior/current-at-cut versions, missing/withheld/closed-source states, exact Finding link and source label after reload. The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

+# S4-08 — Site assumption and consequence history
+
+Status: implementation-ready. Size S: one read and one UI view, one build phase and fresh independent review. Branch for implementation: `task/S4-08-assumption-history` from reviewed main. Planning branch: `plan/S4-08-assumption-history`. Coordinator commits this packet.

+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware and cannot be future. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+

The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

**Pre-S3 planning gates:** `A-claim-work-routes`, `A-actor-identity` and bounded `A-evidence-readiness-health` are recorded in the pre-S3 decisions. `A-finding-index` is resolved by `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`; S4-04 implementation and its fixtures remain open. `A-money-comparability` and broad shared projections remain open. S4-01 and S4-02 consume the route and actor decisions; S6-01 reuses both accepted contracts. These decisions do not add implementation prerequisites to dispatch. At each S2â€“S7 outline refinement, compare the packet with the registry's `queue_baseline_nodes`; a contract or fixture no longer owned by the queue leaves that baseline before marginal estimates are used.

**Simulation and edge decision:** `D-2026-10-07-s2-edge-split-and-demo-source` sets S2-01a/b/c and a simulated-only S2 demo. S2-02 owns authenticated upload and central diagnostics; the first real pilot chooses a real capture or labelled vendor-format fixture for physical protocol proof. `A-edge-batch-signature` defers batch signatures/key rotation; TLS gateway credentials, canonical digest and exact-byte retry remain required. S3-01 owns dispatch scenarios, S6-02 owns two-run verification scenarios, and causal battery-stress production remains outside S7. At each outline refinement, the Planner checks `queue_baseline_nodes` against the refined packet; technical `requires` remain separate from queue order.

The recorded `A-lab-registered-basis`, `A-edge-diagnostics` and `A-demo-scenarios` contracts are consumed by S2-01a/c and later packets. S2-01c owns `I-edge-fault-scenarios`; S3-01 owns `I-sim-dispatch-cases`; S6-02 owns `I-sim-verification-cases`. `A-real-peer-source` records simulated-only demo input; the first real pilot records its physical peer before refining an adapter task.

+# S4-08 — Site assumption and consequence history
+
+Status: implementation-ready. Size S: one read and one UI view, one build phase and fresh independent review. Branch for implementation: `task/S4-08-assumption-history` from reviewed main. Planning branch: `plan/S4-08-assumption-history`. Coordinator commits this packet.
+
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true. Under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`, fresh independent slice review (and fixes) is the merge gate; the owner reviews this screen in the combined W1 walkthrough, not a per-slice session. Registry: `I-assumption-consequence-history-read`, `I-assumption-consequence-history-ui`, `I-S4-08`; core portion `assumptions:3#a` remains open until implementation is reviewed. Authority: `D-2026-10-14-s402-post-review-follow-ons`, `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md` Q4 and `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Outcome and code reality
+
+At one selected recorded cutoff, a Site reader can see its price timeline and every recorded consequence version that used a price, including earlier versions, exact lineage and a link to the owning Finding. No Site or estate sum follows from these rows. The v69 `assumptions` screen (`frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:485-490`) supplies the price/history composition; its editable price, maintenance accrual, preview and illustrative values are not authorized product data.
+
+| Existing seam (unless **new**) | S4-08 work |
+| --- | --- |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/assumption_read.py:3-5,63-96` and `financial_read_api.py:64-85` select a Site price timeline at `recorded_at`, with keyset price paging. S4-06 preserves equal-start succession at prior cutoffs (`financials/assumptions.py:193-217`). | Reuse the exact UTC cutoff and URL selector. Keep price selection independent of consequence paging. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/adapters/postgres_consequence_store.py:381-436` reads a single FindingRevision chain; `financials/consequence_read.py:169-268` exposes exact versions, source provenance and money gates. `finding_api.py:245-268` scopes exact links. | **New** Site-wide, read-only consequence-history query and API; do not stitch the UI from per-Finding requests. Reuse exact row DTO, scope and gate semantics where sound. |
+| `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-123,143-152` stores tenant/Site, Finding/Revision, price link, window and `recorded_at`; main head `0019_verification_core`. | Existing immutable rows and links support this query. No migration unless the implementer demonstrates a missing stored fact or required index with query proof and Architect review. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py:23-36,60-83`, `financials/consequence_read.py:217-254` and `frontend/src/sites/sourceProvenance.tsx:1-94` resolve S4-05b source labels per exact consequence version; LR-01 eligibility and currentness also gate money. | **New** per-row provenance and current-money disposition, using stored accepted-evidence bindings. Never infer source from Site/day, price, or the mock. |
+| `frontend/src/sites/SiteFinancials.tsx:192-231,356-390` preserves Assumptions cutoff/price page in the URL. | **New** consequence-history panel or contextual view reachable from selected price history; maintain the same cutoff through paging, Finding links and reload. |
+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware and cannot be future. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+
+Combined W1 walkthrough map: the existing `assumptions` row in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` now includes the S4-08 guarded case and selected `recorded_at` URL. Show prior/current-at-cut versions, missing/withheld/closed-source states, exact Finding link and source label after reload. The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

+| `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py:23-36,60-83`, `financials/consequence_read.py:217-254` and `frontend/src/sites/sourceProvenance.tsx:1-94` resolve S4-05b source labels per exact consequence version; LR-01 eligibility and currentness also gate money. | **New** per-row provenance and current-money disposition, using stored accepted-evidence bindings. Never infer source from Site/day, price, or the mock. |
+| `frontend/src/sites/SiteFinancials.tsx:192-231,356-390` preserves Assumptions cutoff/price page in the URL. | **New** consequence-history panel or contextual view reachable from selected price history; maintain the same cutoff through paging, Finding links and reload. |
+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware and cannot be future. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+

+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.

+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

const visiting = new Set();
const visited = new Set();
function visit(id) {
  if (visiting.has(id)) fail(`Dependency cycle at ${id}`);
  if (visited.has(id)) return;
  const node = nodes.get(id);
  if (!node) fail(`Unknown node ${id}`);
  visiting.add(id);
  for (const dependency of node.requires) {
    if (!nodes.has(dependency)) fail(`${id}: unknown dependency ${dependency}`);
    if (nodes.get(dependency).kind === 'product') fail(`${id}: product node used as prerequisite`);
    visit(dependency);
  }
  visiting.delete(id);
  visited.add(id);
}
for (const id of nodes.keys()) visit(id);

  ['I-S2-02', ['I-S2-01c', 'I-accepted-evidence-store', 'I-edge-diagnostics-store']],
  ['I-S3-01', ['I-sim-dispatch-cases']],
  ['D-verification-window', ['I-sim-verification-cases']],

const queueVisiting = new Set();
const queueVisited = new Set();
function visitQueue(id) {
  if (queueVisiting.has(id)) fail(`Combined technical/queue cycle at ${id}`);
  if (queueVisited.has(id)) return;
  const node = nodes.get(id);
  if (node.sequenced_after !== undefined && !Array.isArray(node.sequenced_after)) fail(`${id}: sequenced_after must be an array`);
  queueVisiting.add(id);
  for (const dependency of [...node.requires, ...(node.sequenced_after || [])]) {
    if (!nodes.has(dependency) || nodes.get(dependency).kind === 'product') fail(`${id}: invalid queue dependency ${dependency}`);
    visitQueue(dependency);
  }
  queueVisiting.delete(id);
  queueVisited.add(id);
}

  if (nodes.has(node.id)) fail(`Duplicate node ${node.id}`);
  if (!areas.has(node.area)) fail(`${node.id}: unknown area`);
  if (!['architecture', 'implementation', 'data', 'product'].includes(node.kind)) fail(`${node.id}: invalid kind`);
  if (!Array.isArray(node.requires)) fail(`${node.id}: requires must be an array`);
  if (node.kind === 'product') {
    if (node.status !== 'pending owner' && node.status !== 'recorded') fail(`${node.id}: invalid product status`);
    if (!('target_tier' in node) || !('priority' in node) || !Array.isArray(node.core_panels)) fail(`${node.id}: incomplete product decision`);

    if (!['S', 'M', 'L'].includes(node.effort)) fail(`${node.id}: missing effort size`);

Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+
+Combined W1 walkthrough map: the existing `assumptions` row in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` now includes the S4-08 guarded case and selected `recorded_at` URL. Show prior/current-at-cut versions, missing/withheld/closed-source states, exact Finding link and source label after reload. The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.
