Registry: `I-assumption-consequence-history-read`, `I-assumption-consequence-history-ui`, `I-S4-08`; core portion `assumptions:3#a` remains open until implementation is reviewed. Authority: `D-2026-10-14-s402-post-review-follow-ons`, `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md` Q4 and `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Outcome and code reality
+
+At one selected recorded cutoff, a Site reader can see its price timeline and every recorded consequence version that used a price, including earlier versions, exact lineage and a link to the owning Finding. No Site or estate sum follows from these rows. The v69 `assumptions` screen (`frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:485-490`) supplies the price/history composition; its editable price, maintenance accrual, preview and illustrative values are not authorized product data.
+
+| Existing seam (unless **new**) | S4-08 work |
+| --- | --- |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/assumption_read.py:3-5,63-96` and `financial_read_api.py:64-85` select a Site price timeline at `recorded_at`, with keyset price paging. S4-06 preserves equal-start succession at prior cutoffs (`financials/assumptions.py:193-217`). | Reuse the exact UTC cutoff and URL selector. Keep price selection independent of consequence paging. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/adapters/postgres_consequence_store.py:381-436` reads a single FindingRevision chain; `financials/consequence_read.py:169-268` exposes exact versions, source provenance and money gates. `finding_api.py:245-268` scopes exact links. | **New** Site-wide, read-only consequence-history query and API; do not stitch the UI from per-Finding requests. Reuse exact row DTO, scope and gate semantics where sound. |
+| `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-123,143-152` stores tenant/Site, Finding/Revision, price link, window and `recorded_at`; main head `0019_verification_core`. | Existing immutable rows and links support this query. No migration unless the implementer demonstrates a missing stored fact or required index with query proof and Architect review. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py:23-36,60-83`, `financials/consequence_read.py:217-254` and `frontend/src/sites/sourceProvenance.tsx:1-94` resolve S4-05b source labels per exact consequence version; LR-01 eligibility and currentness also gate money. | **New** per-row provenance and current-money disposition, using stored accepted-evidence bindings. Never infer source from Site/day, price, or the mock. |
+| `frontend/src/sites/SiteFinancials.tsx:192-231,356-390` preserves Assumptions cutoff/price page in the URL. | **New** consequence-history panel or contextual view reachable from selected price history; maintain the same cutoff through paging, Finding links and reload. |
+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware and cannot be future. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+
+Combined W1 walkthrough map: the existing `assumptions` row in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` now includes the S4-08 guarded case and selected `recorded_at` URL. Show prior/current-at-cut versions, missing/withheld/closed-source states, exact Finding link and source label after reload. The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

+from pathlib import Path
+import json
+
+path = Path('frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json')
+raw = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+old = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance"],'
+new = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance","I-assumption-consequence-history-read","I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-S4-08"],'
+assert raw.count(old) == 1
+raw = raw.replace(old, new)
+old_task = '"task":"S4-02 Assumptions-history follow-on (unscheduled)"'
+assert raw.count(old_task) == 2
+raw = raw.replace(old_task, '"task":"S4-08"')
+lines = raw.splitlines(keepends=True)
+index = next(i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if '"id":"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui"' in line)
+node = ('    {"id":"I-S4-08","kind":"implementation","layer":"task","area":"money",'
+        '"owner":"Implementer / Reviewer","status":"open","requires":['
+        '"A-S4-financial","I-assumption-consequence-history-read",'
+        '"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-stored-demo-provenance",'
+        '"I-price-equal-start-succession","I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit"],'
+        '"sequenced_after":["I-S7-01b"],"effort":"S","evidence":null,"task":"S4-08",'
+        '"acceptance_target":"Reviewed Site-scoped Assumptions consequence history at the selected recorded cutoff, with exact price/Finding lineage, per-version source and no unsupported current amount; owner visual check deferred to combined W1 walkthrough."},\n')
+lines.insert(index + 1, node)
+raw = ''.join(lines)
+obj = json.loads(raw)
+assert len([n for n in obj['nodes'] if n.get('task') == 'S4-08']) == 3
+path.write_text(raw, encoding='utf-8', newline='')

+# S4-08 — Site assumption and consequence history
+
+Status: implementation-ready. Size S: one read and one UI view, one build phase and fresh independent review. Branch for implementation: `task/S4-08-assumption-history` from reviewed main. Planning branch: `plan/S4-08-assumption-history`. Coordinator commits this packet.
+
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true. Under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`, fresh independent slice review (and fixes) is the merge gate; the owner reviews this screen in the combined W1 walkthrough, not a per-slice session. Registry: `I-assumption-consequence-history-read`, `I-assumption-consequence-history-ui`, `I-S4-08`; core portion `assumptions:3#a` remains open until implementation is reviewed. Authority: `D-2026-10-14-s402-post-review-follow-ons`, `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md` Q4 and `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Outcome and code reality
+
+At one selected recorded cutoff, a Site reader can see its price timeline and every recorded consequence version that used a price, including earlier versions, exact lineage and a link to the owning Finding. No Site or estate sum follows from these rows. The v69 `assumptions` screen (`frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:485-490`) supplies the price/history composition; its editable price, maintenance accrual, preview and illustrative values are not authorized product data.
+
+| Existing seam (unless **new**) | S4-08 work |
+| --- | --- |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/assumption_read.py:3-5,63-96` and `financial_read_api.py:64-85` select a Site price timeline at `recorded_at`, with keyset price paging. S4-06 preserves equal-start succession at prior cutoffs (`financials/assumptions.py:193-217`). | Reuse the exact UTC cutoff and URL selector. Keep price selection independent of consequence paging. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/adapters/postgres_consequence_store.py:381-436` reads a single FindingRevision chain; `financials/consequence_read.py:169-268` exposes exact versions, source provenance and money gates. `finding_api.py:245-268` scopes exact links. | **New** Site-wide, read-only consequence-history query and API; do not stitch the UI from per-Finding requests. Reuse exact row DTO, scope and gate semantics where sound. |
+| `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-123,143-152` stores tenant/Site, Finding/Revision, price link, window and `recorded_at`; main head `0019_verification_core`. | Existing immutable rows and links support this query. No migration unless the implementer demonstrates a missing stored fact or required index with query proof and Architect review. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py:23-36,60-83`, `financials/consequence_read.py:217-254` and `frontend/src/sites/sourceProvenance.tsx:1-94` resolve S4-05b source labels per exact consequence version; LR-01 eligibility and currentness also gate money. | **New** per-row provenance and current-money disposition, using stored accepted-evidence bindings. Never infer source from Site/day, price, or the mock. |
+| `frontend/src/sites/SiteFinancials.tsx:192-231,356-390` preserves Assumptions cutoff/price page in the URL. | **New** consequence-history panel or contextual view reachable from selected price history; maintain the same cutoff through paging, Finding links and reload. |
+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware and cannot be future. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+
+Combined W1 walkthrough map: the existing `assumptions` row in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` now includes the S4-08 guarded case and selected `recorded_at` URL. Show prior/current-at-cut versions, missing/withheld/closed-source states, exact Finding link and source label after reload. The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

+from pathlib import Path
+import json
+
+path = Path('frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json')
+raw = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+old = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance"],'
+new = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance","I-assumption-consequence-history-read","I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-S4-08"],'
+assert raw.count(old) == 1
+raw = raw.replace(old, new)
+old_task = '"task":"S4-02 Assumptions-history follow-on (unscheduled)"'
+assert raw.count(old_task) == 2
+raw = raw.replace(old_task, '"task":"S4-08"')
+lines = raw.splitlines(keepends=True)
+index = next(i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if '"id":"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui"' in line)
+node = ('    {"id":"I-S4-08","kind":"implementation","layer":"task","area":"money",'
+        '"owner":"Implementer / Reviewer","status":"open","requires":['
+        '"A-S4-financial","I-assumption-consequence-history-read",'
+        '"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-stored-demo-provenance",'
+        '"I-price-equal-start-succession","I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit"],'
+        '"sequenced_after":["I-S7-01b"],"effort":"S","evidence":null,"task":"S4-08",'
+        '"acceptance_target":"Reviewed Site-scoped Assumptions consequence history at the selected recorded cutoff, with exact price/Finding lineage, per-version source and no unsupported current amount; owner visual check deferred to combined W1 walkthrough."},\n')
+lines.insert(index + 1, node)
+raw = ''.join(lines)
+obj = json.loads(raw)
+assert len([n for n in obj['nodes'] if n.get('task') == 'S4-08']) == 3
+path.write_text(raw, encoding='utf-8', newline='')

+from pathlib import Path
+import json
+
+path = Path('frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json')
+raw = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+old = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance"],'
+new = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance","I-assumption-consequence-history-read","I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-S4-08"],'
+assert raw.count(old) == 1
+raw = raw.replace(old, new)
+old_task = '"task":"S4-02 Assumptions-history follow-on (unscheduled)"'
+assert raw.count(old_task) == 2
+raw = raw.replace(old_task, '"task":"S4-08"')
+lines = raw.splitlines(keepends=True)
+index = next(i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if '"id":"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui"' in line)
+node = ('    {"id":"I-S4-08","kind":"implementation","layer":"task","area":"money",'
+        '"owner":"Implementer / Reviewer","status":"open","requires":['
+        '"A-S4-financial","I-assumption-consequence-history-read",'
+        '"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-stored-demo-provenance",'
+        '"I-price-equal-start-succession","I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit"],'
+        '"sequenced_after":["I-S7-01b"],"effort":"S","evidence":null,"task":"S4-08",'
+        '"acceptance_target":"Reviewed Site-scoped Assumptions consequence history at the selected recorded cutoff, with exact price/Finding lineage, per-version source and no unsupported current amount; owner visual check deferred to combined W1 walkthrough."},\n')
+lines.insert(index + 1, node)
+raw = ''.join(lines)
+obj = json.loads(raw)
+assert len([n for n in obj['nodes'] if n.get('task') == 'S4-08']) == 3
+path.write_text(raw, encoding='utf-8', newline='')

+# S4-08 — Site assumption and consequence history
+
+Status: implementation-ready. Size S: one read and one UI view, one build phase and fresh independent review. Branch for implementation: `task/S4-08-assumption-history` from reviewed main. Planning branch: `plan/S4-08-assumption-history`. Coordinator commits this packet.
+
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true. Under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`, fresh independent slice review (and fixes) is the merge gate; the owner reviews this screen in the combined W1 walkthrough, not a per-slice session. Registry: `I-assumption-consequence-history-read`, `I-assumption-consequence-history-ui`, `I-S4-08`; core portion `assumptions:3#a` remains open until implementation is reviewed. Authority: `D-2026-10-14-s402-post-review-follow-ons`, `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md` Q4 and `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Outcome and code reality
+
+At one selected recorded cutoff, a Site reader can see its price timeline and every recorded consequence version that used a price, including earlier versions, exact lineage and a link to the owning Finding. No Site or estate sum follows from these rows. The v69 `assumptions` screen (`frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:485-490`) supplies the price/history composition; its editable price, maintenance accrual, preview and illustrative values are not authorized product data.
+
+| Existing seam (unless **new**) | S4-08 work |
+| --- | --- |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/assumption_read.py:3-5,63-96` and `financial_read_api.py:64-85` select a Site price timeline at `recorded_at`, with keyset price paging. S4-06 preserves equal-start succession at prior cutoffs (`financials/assumptions.py:193-217`). | Reuse the exact UTC cutoff and URL selector. Keep price selection independent of consequence paging. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/adapters/postgres_consequence_store.py:381-436` reads a single FindingRevision chain; `financials/consequence_read.py:169-268` exposes exact versions, source provenance and money gates. `finding_api.py:245-268` scopes exact links. | **New** Site-wide, read-only consequence-history query and API; do not stitch the UI from per-Finding requests. Reuse exact row DTO, scope and gate semantics where sound. |
+| `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-123,143-152` stores tenant/Site, Finding/Revision, price link, window and `recorded_at`; main head `0019_verification_core`. | Existing immutable rows and links support this query. No migration unless the implementer demonstrates a missing stored fact or required index with query proof and Architect review. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py:23-36,60-83`, `financials/consequence_read.py:217-254` and `frontend/src/sites/sourceProvenance.tsx:1-94` resolve S4-05b source labels per exact consequence version; LR-01 eligibility and currentness also gate money. | **New** per-row provenance and current-money disposition, using stored accepted-evidence bindings. Never infer source from Site/day, price, or the mock. |
+| `frontend/src/sites/SiteFinancials.tsx:192-231,356-390` preserves Assumptions cutoff/price page in the URL. | **New** consequence-history panel or contextual view reachable from selected price history; maintain the same cutoff through paging, Finding links and reload. |
+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware and cannot be future. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+
+Combined W1 walkthrough map: the existing `assumptions` row in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` now includes the S4-08 guarded case and selected `recorded_at` URL. Show prior/current-at-cut versions, missing/withheld/closed-source states, exact Finding link and source label after reload. The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

+from pathlib import Path
+import json
+
+path = Path('frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json')
+raw = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+old = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance"],'
+new = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance","I-assumption-consequence-history-read","I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-S4-08"],'
+assert raw.count(old) == 1
+raw = raw.replace(old, new)
+old_task = '"task":"S4-02 Assumptions-history follow-on (unscheduled)"'
+assert raw.count(old_task) == 2
+raw = raw.replace(old_task, '"task":"S4-08"')
+lines = raw.splitlines(keepends=True)
+index = next(i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if '"id":"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui"' in line)
+node = ('    {"id":"I-S4-08","kind":"implementation","layer":"task","area":"money",'
+        '"owner":"Implementer / Reviewer","status":"open","requires":['
+        '"A-S4-financial","I-assumption-consequence-history-read",'
+        '"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-stored-demo-provenance",'
+        '"I-price-equal-start-succession","I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit"],'
+        '"sequenced_after":["I-S7-01b"],"effort":"S","evidence":null,"task":"S4-08",'
+        '"acceptance_target":"Reviewed Site-scoped Assumptions consequence history at the selected recorded cutoff, with exact price/Finding lineage, per-version source and no unsupported current amount; owner visual check deferred to combined W1 walkthrough."},\n')
+lines.insert(index + 1, node)
+raw = ''.join(lines)
+obj = json.loads(raw)
+assert len([n for n in obj['nodes'] if n.get('task') == 'S4-08']) == 3
+path.write_text(raw, encoding='utf-8', newline='')

+# S4-08 — Site assumption and consequence history
+
+Status: implementation-ready. Size S: one read and one UI view, one build phase and fresh independent review. Branch for implementation: `task/S4-08-assumption-history` from reviewed main. Planning branch: `plan/S4-08-assumption-history`. Coordinator commits this packet.
+
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true. Under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`, fresh independent slice review (and fixes) is the merge gate; the owner reviews this screen in the combined W1 walkthrough, not a per-slice session. Registry: `I-assumption-consequence-history-read`, `I-assumption-consequence-history-ui`, `I-S4-08`; core portion `assumptions:3#a` remains open until implementation is reviewed. Authority: `D-2026-10-14-s402-post-review-follow-ons`, `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md` Q4 and `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Outcome and code reality
+
+At one selected recorded cutoff, a Site reader can see its price timeline and every recorded consequence version that used a price, including earlier versions, exact lineage and a link to the owning Finding. No Site or estate sum follows from these rows. The v69 `assumptions` screen (`frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:485-490`) supplies the price/history composition; its editable price, maintenance accrual, preview and illustrative values are not authorized product data.
+
+| Existing seam (unless **new**) | S4-08 work |
+| --- | --- |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/assumption_read.py:3-5,63-96` and `financial_read_api.py:64-85` select a Site price timeline at `recorded_at`, with keyset price paging. S4-06 preserves equal-start succession at prior cutoffs (`financials/assumptions.py:193-217`). | Reuse the exact UTC cutoff and URL selector. Keep price selection independent of consequence paging. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/adapters/postgres_consequence_store.py:381-436` reads a single FindingRevision chain; `financials/consequence_read.py:169-268` exposes exact versions, source provenance and money gates. `finding_api.py:245-268` scopes exact links. | **New** Site-wide, read-only consequence-history query and API; do not stitch the UI from per-Finding requests. Reuse exact row DTO, scope and gate semantics where sound. |
+| `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-123,143-152` stores tenant/Site, Finding/Revision, price link, window and `recorded_at`; main head `0019_verification_core`. | Existing immutable rows and links support this query. No migration unless the implementer demonstrates a missing stored fact or required index with query proof and Architect review. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py:23-36,60-83`, `financials/consequence_read.py:217-254` and `frontend/src/sites/sourceProvenance.tsx:1-94` resolve S4-05b source labels per exact consequence version; LR-01 eligibility and currentness also gate money. | **New** per-row provenance and current-money disposition, using stored accepted-evidence bindings. Never infer source from Site/day, price, or the mock. |
+| `frontend/src/sites/SiteFinancials.tsx:192-231,356-390` preserves Assumptions cutoff/price page in the URL. | **New** consequence-history panel or contextual view reachable from selected price history; maintain the same cutoff through paging, Finding links and reload. |
+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware and cannot be future. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+
+Combined W1 walkthrough map: the existing `assumptions` row in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` now includes the S4-08 guarded case and selected `recorded_at` URL. Show prior/current-at-cut versions, missing/withheld/closed-source states, exact Finding link and source label after reload. The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

+from pathlib import Path
+import json
+
+path = Path('frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json')
+raw = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+old = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance"],'
+new = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance","I-assumption-consequence-history-read","I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-S4-08"],'
+assert raw.count(old) == 1
+raw = raw.replace(old, new)
+old_task = '"task":"S4-02 Assumptions-history follow-on (unscheduled)"'
+assert raw.count(old_task) == 2
+raw = raw.replace(old_task, '"task":"S4-08"')
+lines = raw.splitlines(keepends=True)
+index = next(i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if '"id":"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui"' in line)
+node = ('    {"id":"I-S4-08","kind":"implementation","layer":"task","area":"money",'
+        '"owner":"Implementer / Reviewer","status":"open","requires":['
+        '"A-S4-financial","I-assumption-consequence-history-read",'
+        '"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-stored-demo-provenance",'
+        '"I-price-equal-start-succession","I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit"],'
+        '"sequenced_after":["I-S7-01b"],"effort":"S","evidence":null,"task":"S4-08",'
+        '"acceptance_target":"Reviewed Site-scoped Assumptions consequence history at the selected recorded cutoff, with exact price/Finding lineage, per-version source and no unsupported current amount; owner visual check deferred to combined W1 walkthrough."},\n')
+lines.insert(index + 1, node)
+raw = ''.join(lines)
+obj = json.loads(raw)
+assert len([n for n in obj['nodes'] if n.get('task') == 'S4-08']) == 3
+path.write_text(raw, encoding='utf-8', newline='')

+# S4-08 — Site assumption and consequence history
+
+Status: implementation-ready. Size S: one read and one UI view, one build phase and fresh independent review. Branch for implementation: `task/S4-08-assumption-history` from reviewed main. Planning branch: `plan/S4-08-assumption-history`. Coordinator commits this packet.
+
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true. Under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`, fresh independent slice review (and fixes) is the merge gate; the owner reviews this screen in the combined W1 walkthrough, not a per-slice session. Registry: `I-assumption-consequence-history-read`, `I-assumption-consequence-history-ui`, `I-S4-08`; core portion `assumptions:3#a` remains open until implementation is reviewed. Authority: `D-2026-10-14-s402-post-review-follow-ons`, `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md` Q4 and `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Outcome and code reality
+
+At one selected recorded cutoff, a Site reader can see its price timeline and every recorded consequence version that used a price, including earlier versions, exact lineage and a link to the owning Finding. No Site or estate sum follows from these rows. The v69 `assumptions` screen (`frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:485-490`) supplies the price/history composition; its editable price, maintenance accrual, preview and illustrative values are not authorized product data.
+
+| Existing seam (unless **new**) | S4-08 work |
+| --- | --- |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/assumption_read.py:3-5,63-96` and `financial_read_api.py:64-85` select a Site price timeline at `recorded_at`, with keyset price paging. S4-06 preserves equal-start succession at prior cutoffs (`financials/assumptions.py:193-217`). | Reuse the exact UTC cutoff and URL selector. Keep price selection independent of consequence paging. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/adapters/postgres_consequence_store.py:381-436` reads a single FindingRevision chain; `financials/consequence_read.py:169-268` exposes exact versions, source provenance and money gates. `finding_api.py:245-268` scopes exact links. | **New** Site-wide, read-only consequence-history query and API; do not stitch the UI from per-Finding requests. Reuse exact row DTO, scope and gate semantics where sound. |
+| `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-123,143-152` stores tenant/Site, Finding/Revision, price link, window and `recorded_at`; main head `0019_verification_core`. | Existing immutable rows and links support this query. No migration unless the implementer demonstrates a missing stored fact or required index with query proof and Architect review. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py:23-36,60-83`, `financials/consequence_read.py:217-254` and `frontend/src/sites/sourceProvenance.tsx:1-94` resolve S4-05b source labels per exact consequence version; LR-01 eligibility and currentness also gate money. | **New** per-row provenance and current-money disposition, using stored accepted-evidence bindings. Never infer source from Site/day, price, or the mock. |
+| `frontend/src/sites/SiteFinancials.tsx:192-231,356-390` preserves Assumptions cutoff/price page in the URL. | **New** consequence-history panel or contextual view reachable from selected price history; maintain the same cutoff through paging, Finding links and reload. |
+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware. The Assumptions price and consequence requests must share that exact cutoff. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+
+Combined W1 walkthrough map: the existing `assumptions` row in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` now includes the S4-08 guarded case and selected `recorded_at` URL. Show prior/current-at-cut versions, missing/withheld/closed-source states, exact Finding link and source label after reload. The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+`I-S4-08` follows `I-S7-01b` only in the current executable queue's accounting order; S7 is not a technical prerequisite of this history read.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

+from pathlib import Path
+import json
+
+path = Path('frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json')
+raw = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+old = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance"],'
+new = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance","I-assumption-consequence-history-read","I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-S4-08"],'
+assert raw.count(old) == 1
+raw = raw.replace(old, new)
+old_task = '"task":"S4-02 Assumptions-history follow-on (unscheduled)"'
+assert raw.count(old_task) == 2
+raw = raw.replace(old_task, '"task":"S4-08"')
+lines = raw.splitlines(keepends=True)
+index = next(i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if '"id":"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui"' in line)
+node = ('    {"id":"I-S4-08","kind":"implementation","layer":"task","area":"money",'
+        '"owner":"Implementer / Reviewer","status":"open","requires":['
+        '"A-S4-financial","I-assumption-consequence-history-read",'
+        '"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-stored-demo-provenance",'
+        '"I-price-equal-start-succession","I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit"],'
+        '"sequenced_after":["I-S7-01b"],"effort":"S","evidence":null,"task":"S4-08",'
+        '"acceptance_target":"Reviewed Site-scoped Assumptions consequence history at the selected recorded cutoff, with exact price/Finding lineage, per-version source and no unsupported current amount; owner visual check deferred to combined W1 walkthrough."},\n')
+lines.insert(index + 1, node)
+raw = ''.join(lines)
+obj = json.loads(raw)
+assert len([n for n in obj['nodes'] if n.get('task') == 'S4-08']) == 3
+path.write_text(raw, encoding='utf-8', newline='')

Under `.agent/owner-walkthrough-policy-2026-10-16.md`, fresh independent slice review (and fixes) is the merge gate; the owner reviews this screen in the combined W1 walkthrough, not a per-slice session. Registry: `I-assumption-consequence-history-read`, `I-assumption-consequence-history-ui`, `I-S4-08`; core portion `assumptions:3#a` remains open until implementation is reviewed. Authority: `D-2026-10-14-s402-post-review-follow-ons`, `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md` Q4 and `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Outcome and code reality
+
+At one selected recorded cutoff, a Site reader can see its price timeline and every recorded consequence version that used a price, including earlier versions, exact lineage and a link to the owning Finding. No Site or estate sum follows from these rows. The v69 `assumptions` screen (`frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:485-490`) supplies the price/history composition; its editable price, maintenance accrual, preview and illustrative values are not authorized product data.
+
+| Existing seam (unless **new**) | S4-08 work |
+| --- | --- |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/assumption_read.py:3-5,63-96` and `financial_read_api.py:64-85` select a Site price timeline at `recorded_at`, with keyset price paging. S4-06 preserves equal-start succession at prior cutoffs (`financials/assumptions.py:193-217`). | Reuse the exact UTC cutoff and URL selector. Keep price selection independent of consequence paging. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/financials/adapters/postgres_consequence_store.py:381-436` reads a single FindingRevision chain; `financials/consequence_read.py:169-268` exposes exact versions, source provenance and money gates. `finding_api.py:245-268` scopes exact links. | **New** Site-wide, read-only consequence-history query and API; do not stitch the UI from per-Finding requests. Reuse exact row DTO, scope and gate semantics where sound. |
+| `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-123,143-152` stores tenant/Site, Finding/Revision, price link, window and `recorded_at`; main head `0019_verification_core`. | Existing immutable rows and links support this query. No migration unless the implementer demonstrates a missing stored fact or required index with query proof and Architect review. |
+| `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/provenance_read.py:23-36,60-83`, `financials/consequence_read.py:217-254` and `frontend/src/sites/sourceProvenance.tsx:1-94` resolve S4-05b source labels per exact consequence version; LR-01 eligibility and currentness also gate money. | **New** per-row provenance and current-money disposition, using stored accepted-evidence bindings. Never infer source from Site/day, price, or the mock. |
+| `frontend/src/sites/SiteFinancials.tsx:192-231,356-390` preserves Assumptions cutoff/price page in the URL. | **New** consequence-history panel or contextual view reachable from selected price history; maintain the same cutoff through paging, Finding links and reload. |
+
+## Read and UI contract
+
+1. **New** `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/financial-assumptions/consequences?recorded_at={UTC instant}&after={opaque cursor}&limit={1..100}` (exact route name may follow API convention). Missing `recorded_at` selects a server-fixed UTC cutoff returned in the response and placed in next-page/UI URLs; explicit cutoff must be timezone-aware. The Assumptions price and consequence requests must share that exact cutoff. All pages use the *same* cutoff. Query `financial_consequence_revision.recorded_at <= cutoff`, tenant and resolved Site in one repeatable-read snapshot; do not depend on current heads to enumerate old rows. Sort by `(recorded_at DESC, id DESC)`; cursor encodes the last pair and cutoff, is validated for this tenant/Site and limit, and refuses mismatch/tampering as typed 400. Fetch `limit+1`, return `next` only when more exists. Equal timestamps, insertions after cutoff, empty set and final page must neither duplicate nor skip rows. No count is required.
+2. **New** each row carries exact `finding_id`, `finding_revision_id`, `consequence_revision_id`, linked `price_revision_id` or typed absence, consequence `revision_no`, `recorded_at`, Finding/consequence valid window, price assignment valid and recorded intervals when available, consequence state, typed missing or closed-source state, and the exact S4-05b source block for *that version*. Join price via stored consequence-assumption link, never today's price. Preserve every immutable revision, including unpriced rows and prior price revisions; an absent price is not zero. A broken/foreign lineage is a typed unavailable row or integrity refusal, never a substituted link.
+3. `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` means the row is the latest consequence revision for the latest FindingRevision of its Finding recorded by that cutoff; all others are `HISTORICAL_AT_CUTOFF`. This describes what was recorded then, not a valid-at-time physical assertion or today's current amount. Show the cutoff and selected Site above the list. A closed S3 source gate, unresolved source block, pending/stale consequence, LR-01 `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, or non-`CURRENT PRICED` exact head forbids a **current** amount. A closed source hides even recorded amounts, as the existing consequence UI does; other prior priced amounts may appear only as labelled audit history with exact links. Reuse server money-gate results; never compute authorization in React.
+4. Scope before lookup: unresolved/foreign Site or foreign Finding, consequence or price IDs return 404/typed refusal with no row or timing leak. A source gate closed for an otherwise stored row is shown as a typed closed-source state, not an empty Site history. Store/query failure is unavailable, not zero. Provide plain empty, loading, error, prior/current-at-cut and withheld states at 1440 and 390 px. Default copy explains “price changed; the measured result did not” only when supported; no raw IDs, codes or implementation language outside a labelled “How this was worked out” disclosure. Link to the exact Finding and its selected consequence, with a contextual deeper destination at both widths per `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux`.
+
+## Build, proof and handoff
+
+One build phase: implement the read, route, client and Assumptions view, then produce a guarded private walkthrough case and app-versus-v69 captures at 1440/390. Test one Site with two price versions (including S4-06 equal-start succession), two Findings, multiple consequence revisions, unpriced and withheld versions, mixed/unknown and closed sources. Assert cutoff immediately before/at/after each recorded timestamp, price validity boundaries, same-timestamp paging with more than one page, reload and exact links; cross-tenant/Site and foreign ID refusal; no current amount during LR-01 pending/withheld or S4-05b mixed/unknown; no amount for closed source. A late appended version after fixed cutoff must not enter page two. Verify 0-record honest state and missing-price state. Run focused backend/frontend tests, typecheck, one final full backend and frontend suite per `backend/TESTING.md`, rule-8 writer guard and registry checker, then fresh independent review. Record commands/results and known limits in `.agent/S4-08-review-packet.md`. A read-only query adds no writer; if implementation adds any write, register and prove its rule-8 guard before review.
+
+Combined W1 walkthrough map: the existing `assumptions` row in `tasks/W1-UX-mockup-fidelity.md` now includes the S4-08 guarded case and selected `recorded_at` URL. Show prior/current-at-cut versions, missing/withheld/closed-source states, exact Finding link and source label after reload. The coordinator's final `.agent/W1-UX-combined-owner-walkthrough.md` records tool-printed URLs, source qualification and 1440/390 captures. Do not promote `assumptions:3#a` before the accepted independent implementation review; record owner walkthrough deferral on promotion.
+
+`I-S4-08` follows `I-S7-01b` only in the current executable queue's accounting order; S7 is not a technical prerequisite of this history read.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. Should the list include unpriced consequence versions with no price link? **Recommend yes:** they are part of Site interpretation history and need an explicit missing-price state.
+2. Does `CURRENT_AT_CUTOFF` require both the latest FindingRevision and latest consequence within it? **Recommend yes:** a newer FindingRevision must demote all older Finding versions, even when their consequence chain has its own head.
+3. Should a selected past cutoff ever show a “current amount”? **Recommend no:** it is audit history; only today's exact-head read may make a current-money claim after every existing gate passes.

+from pathlib import Path
+import json
+
+path = Path('frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json')
+raw = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+old = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance"],'
+new = '"I-S4-05a","I-stored-demo-provenance","I-assumption-consequence-history-read","I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-S4-08"],'
+assert raw.count(old) == 1
+raw = raw.replace(old, new)
+old_task = '"task":"S4-02 Assumptions-history follow-on (unscheduled)"'
+assert raw.count(old_task) == 2
+raw = raw.replace(old_task, '"task":"S4-08"')
+lines = raw.splitlines(keepends=True)
+index = next(i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if '"id":"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui"' in line)
+node = ('    {"id":"I-S4-08","kind":"implementation","layer":"task","area":"money",'
+        '"owner":"Implementer / Reviewer","status":"open","requires":['
+        '"A-S4-financial","I-assumption-consequence-history-read",'
+        '"I-assumption-consequence-history-ui","I-stored-demo-provenance",'
+        '"I-price-equal-start-succession","I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit"],'
+        '"sequenced_after":["I-S7-01b"],"effort":"S","evidence":null,"task":"S4-08",'
+        '"acceptance_target":"Reviewed Site-scoped Assumptions consequence history at the selected recorded cutoff, with exact price/Finding lineage, per-version source and no unsupported current amount; owner visual check deferred to combined W1 walkthrough."},\n')
+lines.insert(index + 1, node)
+raw = ''.join(lines)
+obj = json.loads(raw)
+assert len([n for n in obj['nodes'] if n.get('task') == 'S4-08']) == 3
+path.write_text(raw, encoding='utf-8', newline='')
